Nonetheless, leaders within the testing sector will standout among competition, with
greater sales CAGR comparing to the industry.
We see HTSI's sales for the next few years to mainly be driven by 1) thermal control
module shipment and 2) CPO equipment shipment. In the past, thermal control

modules weren't necessary for chip probing, due to less TDP for the chips. However, as
new iterations of AI accelerators now got substantial power draw, this unlocks a brand
new TAM for HTSI to enter with their thermal control modules to pair with probers, in
order to prevent probe card damages.
 Robust thermal control module shipments.  We estimate HTSI to ship
3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2027E/28E, with continuous demand in
future years, bringing their 2026-31E semiconductor equipment sales CAGR to

48%.

### Page 4

 Thermal control module ASP expansion.  Gen 2 thermal control modules will
start to ship in 2027E which will drive tool ASP up around 25-50%.
 Demand supported by Advantest expansions. We believe the 3,000 thermal
control modules shipment forecast for 2027E is not far-fetched, as we read-across
from Advantest's announced capacity expansion plan (testers will be paired with
probers with a 1:1 ratio) with increasing capacity up to 5,000 testers within

FY2027/3 and they also announced a 10,000-unit target within CY2028.
Although the testers capacity expansion plan includes testers for final test, we do
see a good portion to be allocated to chip probing given shift-left testing trend
increasing both testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CPO is another brand new TAM for semiconductor testing.  As insertion 2
and 3 testing equipment demand starts to rise in 2027 with scale-out CPO

adoption, we see HTSI to ship 40/70 insertion 2 and 3 equipment in 2027E, with
this number growing in 2028E to 80/160 and higher in future years as CPO
proliferates, and driving 52% 2026-31E test equipment & engineering services
sales CAGR.
 CPO to see content increase. Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment may start to ship in
2027E, with higher throughput and tool ASP .
 Insertion 2 might scale down, but not happening anytime soon. Insertion 2
equipment shipment might come down in 2029E and beyond, as foundries scale-

up yield for photonic IC (PIC) in order to lessen the time consuming insertion 2
testing burden.

Robust growth drivers across semiconductor testing
We believe HTSI is set to benefit from several semiconductor testing growth drivers and
capture the AI induced, expanding testing TAM. We see HTSI is uniquely positioned as
the main beneficiary of two of the strongest growth drivers within semiconductor
testing: testing shift-left and CPO.
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing

### Page 5

The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging.
Figure 4: CP is set to grow substantially with shift-left, each packaging step set to be MORE costly than the prior step

RDL / RWAT testing Wafer-level CP (WLCP)
CoWChiplet (if use SoIC) CoW
CP (Die A)
CP (Die B)
Assembly Test (AST)
oS
Source: UBS
The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while
defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed

testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million
(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall

integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of
wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected by the industry to surge another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level

chip probe (WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and
higher power densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield
degradation, creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal-control
solutions.

Hermes Testing (HTSI) is a key beneficiary of this trend. Leveraging its long-standing
relationship with TSMC and expertise in semiconductor test solutions, HTSI supplies

advanced thermal control modules used alongside leading-edge probers (made by
Tokyo Electron), particularly for AI applications. These thermal control modules are
required for high TDP semiconductor wafer probe in order to maintain the temperature

### Page 6

of the chuck to avoid needle burns or probe card damages. We estimate HTSI will ship
approximately 3,000 thermal-management modules in 2027E, up from 1,200 in 2026E,
supporting sales growth of 127%/152% in 2026E/27E. HTSI is also the partner for
prober installation, prober maintenance and services. In addition, HTSI provides high-
end chuck processing services, an area that should benefit from increasing package sizes
and higher wafer warpage associated with advanced packaging.

Beyond thermal control modules, HTSI also supplies consumables and customised
solutions for wafer-level testing, including probe card cleaning sheets and laser-based
probe head cleaning systems. As probing intensity increases under the shift-left testing
model, demand for cleaning and maintenance solutions should rise alongside probe
card utilisation. Currently, cleaning sheets is around 5% of sales, but we believe it could
gain traction in 2027/28E and beyond. We see additional upside from future modular

laser cleaning systems that could be integrated directly with probers, further expanding
HTSI's content opportunity within advanced wafer-level testing.
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor testing
As interconnect bottlenecks increasingly shift from the memory wall to the copper wall,
we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) adoption is inevitable over the longer term. Testing
remains one of the key bottlenecks to CPO deployment, as optical validation differs

fundamentally from the electrical testing processes that have dominated semiconductor
manufacturing for decades. As a result, CPO is creating a new testing opportunity across
the semiconductor equipment and consumables ecosystem. We have written
extensively on CPO and opportunities that it brings to the semiconductor supply chain,
see our CPO deep dive , our thoughts on CPO timing , 2026 SiPhIA conference
takeaways, and SEMICON 2026 takeaways.
Various test equipment suppliers are scrambling to come up with ideal testing

equipment across the four insertions of CPO testing, with solutions to ensure both
optical and electrical signal integrity of the CPO solutions. The four insertions are:
 Insertion 1: Electronic IC (EIC)/Photonic IC (PIC) test.  Single-side wafer test
that has dedicated probe cards to test the fabricated EIC/PIC wafers.
 Insertion 2: Optical/Electrical test. Double sided wafer test, testing the hybrid
bonded (e.g. via TSMC's COUPE) EIC and PIC wafers by an opto-electrical test that

verifies the electrical signals on the top and optical signal on the bottom of the
hybrid bonded wafers.
 Insertion 3: Optical engine (OE) test.  The OE tests are done to ensure the
quality of the OEs and with FAU (Fiber Array Unit) attached, to ensure the signal
integrity of the OEs. The testing equipment needs to be able to handle 224G per
channel data rates, requiring high unit per hour (UPH) throughput, meticulous dirt
mitigation, and maintaining a near 100% contact rating on high-speed sockets.

 Insertion 4: ASIC final test.  This final test step is established post the OEs are
packaged together with the ASIC. The complete ASIC package (now with CPO) is
now tested for opto-electrical functionalities.
As it stands, insertion 2 and insertion 3 are the two areas where there are the most
debates in the industry. Insertion 3 suppliers such as Chroma has noted the trend of
“shift-to-middle” paradigm centered on insertion 3. In high-density scale-out switches

deploying up to 32 optical engines (e.g. Nvidia’s Spectrum-X architectures), any OE
defect results in package scrap at the CoWoS level, making 100% known good die
(KGD) validation at the singulated OE stage an absolute commercial requirement.

With its 25 years of precision test equipment expertise, ficonTEC is an early mover for
CPO test equipment and has key know-how for optical alignment, which is
quintessential for optical test. HTSI, through its partnership with ficonTEC, is set to be a
major beneficiary once CPO proliferates in 2027E-28E. As the partner of ficonTEC,
Hermes Testing is the key enabler that can help ficonTEC achieve HVM for its CPO
testing equipment. As of now, our view is that both insertion 2 and insertion 3 should be

required within the next two years, despite the difficulties surrounding double-sided
wafer probing (insertion 2), as the yield for OEs are still not high enough to avoid
defective dies. Finding out known-good OEs will be crucial before the FAU coupling
process during the latter stages of CPO packaging, to prevent impeding the final
package with multiple OEs attached to the switch ASIC (e.g. 32 OEs on Nvidia's
Spectrum-X). We estimate Hermes Testing will ship 40/70 units of insertion 2/3 testing

equipment in 2027E, with potential upside to the numbers as we view Hermes Testing
will likely assist with the ramp up for Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment in 2027E. Gen 2
insertion equipment will come with better throughput and ASP .

creating an additional long-term growth opportunity as CPO adoption broadens. While
near-term testing growth is driven by AI accelerators, CPO represents the next major
expansion cycle for semiconductor testing. We believe HTSI is uniquely positioned to

source of incremental revenue beginning in 2027E.

HTSI's unique position for new TAM of thermal control
modules and CPO test equipment
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan, as a spin-off
from Hermes-Epitek. The company provides semiconductor testing and inspection

solutions, engineering services, customized equipment, and test consumables. HTSI
maintains a global service footprint across Taiwan (Hsinchu, Tainan), Mainland China
(Suzhou), Japan (Tokyo, Kumamoto), Singapore, Malaysia, and the United States
(Arizona), enabling close support for leading semiconductor customers.
Its main business segments are structured around three pillars:
 Semiconductor equipment and customized products : HTSI utilises a dual-

track strategy of independent R&D and also equipment agency sales. They offers

### Page 9

highly customized solutions designed to address specific client pain points and
bottlenecks. Key products include: thermal control modules that pair with a 1:1
ratio with wafer-level test probers, electron beam inspection equipment, white
light interference optical inspection systems, laser probe cleaning machines, and
the SYMPHONY fluxless reflow system.
 Testing equipment engineering services:  The company operates the largest
engineering service team in Taiwan, consisting of over 340 technical professionals.

The engineering service team provides extensive equipment life-cycle support,
including installation, relocations, modification, testing, routine maintenance, and
repair services. CPO related equipment sales/services will be recognized within this
segment, as CPO are not direct HTSI products, but rather sales recognition via its
partnership with ficonTEC.
 Probe card design, manufacturing, and cleaning materials:  HTSI delivers a
comprehensive product portfolio that supports the entire probe card life-cycle. For

probe cards, they supply membrane, cantilever, and vertical probe cards. As they
are small in scale, HTSI targets niche probe card applications such as RF, 5G/6G,
millimeter wave, LCD driver, low end logics, TIA (Transimpedance Amplifier), etc. In
addition to the three existing probe technologies, HTSI also collaborates with MJC
in Japan for MEMS probe development, targeting sales in 2028E for high-end, AI/
HPC related applications. Cleaning materials is another important business within

this segment, as HTSI source and process sand paper or other materials to offer
cleaning sheets to clean the probe cards, removing oxidations, unwanted particles
and substances that accumulated on the probe cards during the testing process.
The sales resiliency in the coming years mainly stems from its robust relationship with key
industry players, especially as an engineering and service provider with TSMC for testing
solutions. This sets up a solid foundation for its customized equipment business, as they

know the specific testing bottleneck to tackle. For instance, as Hermes-Epitek
manufacturers the probers for Tokyo Electron (TEL) and HTSI helps with installation,
services, and maintenance of the probers, they understand the thermal bottlenecks that
the wafer-level test industry has been facing and can effectively come up with module
solutions to solve this.
Margins set to expand with value-added products and services
The company has demonstrated strong execution in advanced wafer-level testing, with

H126 revenue of NT$2.2bn already exceeding 90% of FY25 revenue (NT$2.4bn).
Profitability has also improved significantly, with GM increasing from 42.1% in 2025 to
54.9% in H126.
With share of value-added products and services set to rise in 2027E in sales mix, HTSI is
set to see GM and operating leverage expansion. As a niche engineering service provider
that has robust ties to leading foundries and OSATs, we believe HTSI has substantial
pricing power and estimate HTSI' GM for thermal control modules to be around 65%,

with GM for CPO equipment of around 60% as HTSI provides an engineering-service-
like business for it. We believe these factors will help the company to reach a 60% long-
term GM range.
OPM is also set to expand with customized equipment sales rise in total sales mix. If we
exclude the one-time expense to be recognized during Q326 for employee stock
benefits (we estimate around NT$1bn), YoY opex growth will be around 99% for
2027E, with downside risk if the company conducts tight cost control measures. We

forecast the company's OPM will expand from 19.5% in 2026E, to 44.3%/47.0% in
2027/28E.

Source: LSEG, UBS
Despite a 264% YTD share-price increase, HTSI still trades at just 15x 2028E P/E. We
believe market expectations for 2027E CPO testing equipment shipments remain
conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on our industry analysis, we
estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, this should
be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3
shipment due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO

adoption expands. Our reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price
implies only a 17% long-term sales CAGR vs our estimate of 27% and long-term GM
level of 33% vs our estimate of 61%. We see scope for re-rating.
We believe the market is not fully reflecting three key growth drivers:
1) Shift-left testing is still underappreciated.
As advanced packaging costs rise and AI accelerators become increasingly complex,
more thorough testing is done at the wafer-level. This should drive sustained demand

for HTSI's thermal control modules, probe card consumables, and other wafer-level
testing solutions.
2) CPO revenue potential remains underestimated.
Investor expectations for 2027 CPO equipment shipments, particularly insertion 2
testing systems, remain conservative. Based on our industry studies, we forecast HTSI
will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, with further potential
upside in 2028E as CPO adoption broadens.
3) New products provide additional optionality.

Beyond its core businesses, HTSI is expanding into higher-value solutions including mass-
reflow tools, MEMS probe cards and laser cleaning systems, creating incremental
growth opportunities that we believe are largely absent from current expectations.
We value HTSI using 31x average 2028E PE, underpinned by a 32% 2027-31E earnings
CAGR. We use 2028E PE as our view is that 2028 is the year for thermal control modules
and CPO adoption grow from the initial ramping up stage to mass adoption stage. We

believe using handlers as a peer group is justifiable for a unique company like HTSI, with
thermal control modules for chip probing sharing similar characteristics as active thermal
control used for final test, in addition to Hon Precision and Chroma having exposure to
CPO test equipment. For equipment vendors, the peer group's average NTM PE post
2022 is around 35x, and the group is currently trading at around 41x NTM PE (see
What's Priced In chart).

HTSI is trading at NT$4,190 as of 6
Oct 2026

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is heavily skewed (2.2:1) to the upside.
UPSIDE (NT$10,000): Our upside scenario reflects stronger than expected thermal
module and CPO equipment shipment in 2027-28E, with additional upside from better
traction of probe cards and mass reflow tools, forming 157%/91% YoY sales growth in
2027/28E and even higher operating leverage with OPM of 46.3%/50.0% in 2027/28E.

This scenario implies a 34x 2028E PE, a upside driving by more equipment demand
stemming for robust shift-left test induced equipment bottleneck, alongside CPO
proliferation with scale-up CPO equipment pull-in in 2028E.
BASE (NT$8,880): Our price target of NT$8,880 is based on 31x 2028E PE. We expect an
earnings CAGR of 32% in 2027-31E. With shift-left trend for more thermal module
requirement during testing, alongside 40/70 CPO insertion 2/3 shipment. We see these

growth drivers driving YoY sales growth from a high base of 127% in 2026E to
152%/78% YoY in 2027/2028E, with OPM of 44.3%/47.0% in 2027/28E.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,500): Our downside scenario reflects lower shipment of thermal
control modules due to less advanced prober demand with less insertion 2 and 3
equipment shipment due to bottlenecks with CPO production, alongside limited
additional growth drivers to be picked up in 2028 such as MEMS probe card and mass

reflow tools. This leads to weaker YoY sales growth of 67%/50% in 2027/28E and
operating margin of 29.3%/32.0% in 2027/28E as equipment shipment fails to scale.
This scenario implies a 14x 2028E PE, as valuation de-rate with dimmed outlook without
CPO and limited upside from thermal control module shipments.

Company Description
Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu,
Taiwan. It focuses on providing semiconductor testing and
inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical support,
and maintenance services. Its main business segments are
customized semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and
probe cards and other materials.
Industry Outlook
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC

applications, are set to increase in both front-end wafer
fabrication advancements and back-end packaging complexities
with each iterations. With the industry now moves to utilize
"shift left", chip probing and its relevant supply chain will be key
beneficiaries of this trend which we believe will unlock multi-
year, secular growth trends.

Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It focuses on
providing semiconductor testing and inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical
support, and maintenance services. Its main business segments are customized
semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and probe cards and other materials.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Hermes Testing based on PE methodology.
Risks for the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in
the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development

such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech
companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected front-end/CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in
Hermes Testing gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from

either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in
Hermes Testing losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could
lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to
Hermes Testing, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of

the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact Hermes
Testing’s margins for equipment shipments.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 146: 8046南電_1151001_BofA

### Page 1

NYPCB

Alarm cleared with new drivers into 2027; 
re-it. Buy with PO raise to NT$2,000

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,000 TWD | Price: 1,340 TWD

BT substrate price hike resumed from 4Q26 
Following (see report) the worries of moderating BT substrate price hike in early 
September, we now expect NYPCB to still raise relevant ASP by single-digit % in 4Q26, 
and the hike is purely driven by favorable S/D which will fully translate into the accretion 
in bottom-line. Following this, BT substrate price will likely be raised by another 20+% in 
1Q27, due partly to the cost pass-through. We currently bake in ~10% revenue QoQ with 
32-33% GM for 4Q26, but we do not rule out the possibility for its revenue to surprise 
on the upside, should there be a faster ramp of its ABF substrate capacity in Kunshan. 
New driver for 2027: server CPU & mix optimization  
Looking into 2027 we expect the benefits of demand overflow on (see report) server 
CPU side to materialize, and we flag a chance for the firm to record 20+% revenue from 
the emerging business with AMD server CPU, where NYPCB is working on the 
qualification in its Shulin site currently and should capture most of the spillover demand 
if the progress goes smooth. Meanwhile, the firm will likely allocate more capacity from 
other sites (e.g. Kunshan) to fulfill the demand from Broadcom, which also helps enhance 
the mix. Currently we model ~50% GM for its ABF substrate business in 2027, but we 
see chance for further upward revision, driven by more favorable S/D dynamics and mix.  
Raise estimates/PO; prime beneficiary of price hike 
NYPCB’s share price (up ~50%) lags its major peers Unimicron/Kinsus (up ~70%) since 
late-July, but in our view it is still the best proxy to position for price hike in the 
substrate market. Judging from (see report) Kinsus’ August prints last week, we believe 
NYPCB remains on track to meet or beat the market’s expectations on N-T margins, and 
we reiterate Buy on the stock. Along with 13%/18% EPS raise in 2027/28E thanks to the 
more benign pricing/margin outlook driven by further mix improvement and S/D, we 
raise PO to NT$2,000 (from NT$1,700) same based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1997, NYPCB was listed on the Taiwan Stock 
Exchange in 2006. The firm is a 67%-owned subsidiary of 
Nan Ya Plastics Corporation (1303 TT). NYPCB's main 
products are HDI, PCB and IC substrates (including ABF and 
BT substrates), widely used in telecommunication, 
consumers, PC, and Auto applications. The clients include 
AMD, Broadcom, Samsung, Apple, Continental, etc. The 
main competitors include Unimicron, Kinsus, Ibiden, 
Shinko, and SEMCO, etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on NYPCB, in view of its continuous 
improvement of utilization rate and gross margin across 
ABF and BT substrate business into 2027, thanks to the 
robust demand growth from fabless customers and 
demand overflow in AI ASICs.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
NYPCB (NANYF) 
Our PO of NT$2,000 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E. We view this multiple as fair 
given ROE and operating margin expansion to 39% and 41% in 2027-28E (vs 13% and 
14% in 2025-26E).

Upside risks to our PO are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) higher SiP/AiP adoption, and (3) 
less intensified competition pressure in PCB business.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) muted SiP/AiP adoption, 
and (3) more severe competition in PCB business.

宏捷科 (8086 TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 展望保守 ， 但1H27 有機會轉佳 ， 評等為Trading Buy 。 
投資建議 ：由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26
獲利表現不易較 2Q26 明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續
上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，
故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：受惠於手機相關產品
出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，不
過 ， 受到磊晶片 、 黃金等原材料漲價影響 ， 毛利率降低至29.98% ，
營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳： 展望 2H26 、2027
年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成長空
間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面
對 2026 年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊
而降溫的負面大環境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國
中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩
固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓國旗艦機滲透率
開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router 
Wi-Fi PA 訂單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加， (2) 無人機 PA 
1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ， 由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，
後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市
場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi PA 陸續打入中
國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦
機、高階手機舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲
幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影
響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率即有機
會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就溫室氣體管理策略而言，宏捷科的方向主要有六， (1) 配合政府環保政策
執行溫室氣體盤查， (2) 將空壓機安裝無耗氣排水器，減少運作電能消耗，
(3) 廠房照明設計以自然照明替代電能，並將高耗電之傳統燈具改為 LED 之
省電燈具 (持續改善中 )， (4) 廠內設備逐步汰換 IE3 等級以上之高效能馬達，
(5) 空壓機更換效能較佳的機台及降載， (6) 無塵室氣流循環驅動及空氣過濾
設備降載節能。  
再就溫室氣體排放減量之預算與計畫而言，宏捷科落實的途徑主要有五， (1)
生產程序優化， (2) 廠內節能措施進行， (3) 電力照明之替代及管理， (4) 馬達
汰舊換新 (IE3 等級以上 )， (5) 增設含氟排氣處理設備。  
至於宏捷科溫室氣體排放量減量目標則為針對電能減量以降低間接排放之
溫室氣體數量、減少 PFCs 氣體排放以減少直接排放之溫室氣體數量。  
手機相關產品出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：  
宏捷科為砷化鎵晶圓代工廠，目前總產能為 2 萬片 /月 (以 6 吋晶圓為計算基
礎 )，僅次於同業穩懋 (3105 TT) 的第二大廠，但就現有無塵室空間而言，最
大產能規模可達 4 萬片 /月，換言之，在市場需求增溫時，宏捷科僅需購置、
安裝機台，並通過客戶認證 (部份客戶甚至未要求認證 )後，即可擴大產能規
模。  
宏捷科的產品線包括 PA(power amplifier ，功率放大器 )、 switch/LNA( 開關
/low noise amplifier 低雜訊放大器 )等射頻 (RF，radio frequency) 產品，其中，
PA 多以 HBT 製程生產， switch/LNA 則多以 HEMT 製程生產，此外，宏捷
科亦生產光電 (optical) 產品線，主要為供 3D 感測光源之用的 VCSEL 。若再
就各產品線的終端應用而言，訂單則多來自 Wi-Fi 、手機 (phone) ，近幾年營
收比重分別為 30~60% 、 35~75% 。  
中美晶 (5483 TT) 於 08/2020 參與宏捷科的私募，宏捷科除與中美晶共同進
軍 GaN( 氮化鎵 )領域外，並可藉所取得的資金投入研發，至於中美晶則得以
在宏捷科量產的過程中供應自身生產的 GaN on SiC( 碳化矽基氮化鎵 )材料，
亦即雙方合作將發揮垂直整合綜效。  
2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，原因在於手機相關產品營收增
加至 9.49 億元， QoQ+7.55% ，不過，受到磊晶片 (epi wafer) 、黃金等原材料
漲價影響，毛利率降低至 29.98%(1Q26 為 34.63%) ，營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，
稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
1H26 宏捷科營收 25.21 億元，為歷史次高， YoY+44.36% ，其中，手機相關
產品營收 18.32 億元， YoY+56.16% ，原因有二， (1) 中國手機 PA 廠打入韓
國中階手機供應鏈，且自 2026 年開始大量出貨 (過去中、高階手機用 PA 多
由美國業者供應 )， (2) 中國手機 PA 廠供中國手機品牌業者高階機種使用的
產品多仍交由宏捷科代工 (搭載於中、低階手機的 PA 則已積極轉單至中國
同業 )。  
2H26 毛利率仍受原物料成本偏高影響，但 1H27 獲利展望有機會轉佳：  
展望 2H26 、 2027 年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 2 日

3 群益投顧 
長空間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面對 2026
年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊而降溫的負面大環
境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機
高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓
國旗艦機滲透率開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router Wi-Fi PA 訂
單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加 ，(2) 無人機 PA 1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ，
由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美
國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi 
PA 陸續打入中國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。
最後，在新產品的部份，包括 datacom 用 VCSEL( 後續亦將開發 PD 及更高
波長的產品 )、濾波器、消費型 LiDAR( 終端應用如車用、掃地機器人、割草
機等 )均已量產， solar cell 則將於 2026 年年底量產，惟對宏捷科的營收貢獻
程度則均尚待觀察。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦機、高階手機
舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛
利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預
估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。  
雖 2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳，群益對宏捷科的投資建議
為 Trading Buy ：  
由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26 獲利表現不易較 2Q26
明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會
回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Source: Bloomberg
Wired for growth
Initiate coverage with an Outperform rating and target price of NT$270
ACES is entering a two-engine growth phase. It is a key power cable supplier to
Bloom Energy as Bloom doubles its fuel-cell capacity, and it is expanding its high-
speed connector content from AI server daughterboards to ASIC motherboards, as

a high-speed cable leg comes on stream. We forecast a 72% EPS Cagr over 2026-
28, with OPM expanding from 7.4% in 2026 to 11.0% in 2027. Our TP of NT$270
is based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, and we initiate coverage at Outperform.
Bloom Energy the key earnings driver in 2027
ACES's multiyear partnership with Bloom locks in its share as Bloom plans to lift
capacity from 2GW in 2026 to 4GW/6GW in 2027/28, based on our checks. ACES
has shipped fuel-cell power cables to Bloom since 1Q26 and will add external DLO

high-voltage cables, with ASP of US$80-100k per set. We expect Bloom power
cables to be NT$5.8bn of sales and account for 28% of group revenue in 2027.
Power products need little incremental R&D and benefit from strong operating
leverage. We expect overall OPM to expand from 7.4% in 2026 to 11.0% in 2027.
High-speed products a new driver
Previously, ACES supplied only daughterboard connectors, at 10-20 units per rack.
On ASIC platforms, where ODMs have more say in supplier selection, ACES has

secured the opportunity to ship both motherboard and daughterboard connectors
(about 30 units per rack) and broadens its connector product offerings. With Gen6
connectors carrying around a 15% ASP premium over Gen5, and ACES as one of
only four vendors qualified by CSPs at Gen6, we forecast server revenue growth of
107%/74% YoY in 2026/27. The newly added Gen6 PCIe cable product, qualified
by two CSPs, began shipping in April 2026 at 20% of GM, with room to rise towards

30% as yield improves. Hence, we expect GM to recover to 26% in 2027.
Capacity in place ahead of demand
ACES has committed firm capacity ahead of demand. For power cables, it has leased
a new facility next to its Philippine plant, with new capacity planned to come online
in 4Q26. For server products, it plans to raise connector capacity from 4m to 5m
units/mo and will expand high-speed cable production lines from two to eight.
Initiate coverage with an Outperform rating

We forecast 2027/28SP EPS of NT$11.19/NT$14.93, with our 2027 forecast 48%
ahead of consensus. Our TP of NT$270 is based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, backed
by a 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP driven by power cable ramp-up and high-speed
connector shipment growth. We initiate coverage with an Outperform rating.

Bloom Energy the key earnings driver in 2027
We see tremendous advantages for ACES from Bloom Energy’s strong demand backed
by its aggressive expansion plan. ACES supplies inner power cables used inside each
fuel cell in 2026 and will begin supplying external diesel locomotive (DLO) cables that
connect fuel cell stacks into a fuel cell module (energy server). Each set (including inner
cable and external cable) carries dollar content of US$80-100k.

Furthermore, ACES has worked with Bloom on high-voltage power cables for SOFC
for around six years, even though the real contribution did not transpire until 2026. On
the back of this long-standing relationship, ACES is the key supplier during the
development phase and holds at least a 30% share in the mass production stage.
Together with these considerations, we expect inner cables to contribute revenue of
NT$5,113m/NT$8,074m in 2027/28, while external cables will add revenue of

NT$707m/NT$1,154m.
Figure 1
Power cable revenue from Bloom Energy and YoY change

As power scarcity remains the key bottleneck for AI datacentre construction, Bloom
serves as a suitable solution and offers fuel cell systems that generate electricity from
natural gas. Its modular, on-site, 24/7 power platform stands apart in the power sector.
Against a conventional grid built on centralised generation, Bloom delivers compelling
time-to-power, cleaner air and water emissions profile, native-DC compatibility with

emerging 800V AI datacentre architectures, and a solution that communities and
nearby residents accept more readily.

infrastructure, with Bloom providing its fuel cell-based power generation systems and

solution, signing a 10-year master agreement worth up to $2.6bn. The first phase
covers 328MW of installed capacity, or roughly 250MW of guaranteed capacity, and
is expected to come online within 2026. Bloom also expanded its Oracle contract from
an initial 1.2GW to up to 2.8GW and signed deals including AEP (up to 1 GW) and
Equinix (over 100 MW across 19 datacentres), broadening its customer base materially.
Wide range of power cable products
ACES supplies Bloom with a variety of power cables across different specifications,

including Power Conversion System (PCS) cables, hot box cables and Diesel
Locomotive (DLO) high-voltage cables, rather than a single product type. In 2026,
ACES only supplies PCS cables and hot box cables, while the company will expand its
product offerings to DLO high-voltage cables from 2027, which we expect to
contribute another NT$707m of sales for the whole year.
The PCS cables and hot box cables are the inner power cables. Hot box cables
interconnect individual fuel cells within the hot box, where power generation creates

significant heat and therefore requires materials with higher durability and heat
resistance. Kaori and MTAR supply hot boxes for fuel cell power generation systems,
which require ACES’s hot box cables as one of their key components.
On the other hand, PCS cables are installed inside the Power Conversion System (PCS)
and are supplied to AcBel, a PCS solution provider. The PCS converts DC output into
usable power and manages load changes. As SOFCs generate DC power with voltage

varying according to load, the output cannot be directly connected to a datacentre.
The external power cable portfolio is mainly DLO high-voltage cables, which transmit
high-voltage DC power between fuel cell platforms, power modules and microgrid
systems. As external cables need to withstand higher voltage levels, their dollar content
is expected to be slightly higher than that of inner power cables.
Lastly, ACES also ships cables directly to Bloom Energy for development purposes.

DLO high-voltage cable opportunity heading into 800V DC era
We also see DLO cables, which are currently used outside the fuel cell, as having
significant potential after introducing 800V architecture. Under conventional AC
architecture, grid power, onsite turbines and reciprocating engines supply AC

power, and the downstream transformer, switchgear and UPS cables belong to the
utility/EPC supply chain, which ACES does not address. Bloom can deliver native
DC power directly to the rack, so the DC output cables might become part of
Bloom’s system. We see this opening as a new content opportunity for ACES as a
qualified high-voltage power cable supplier.
Within Bloom’s energy server under 800V architecture, the longer series stack of
fuel cells and the replacement of the inverter with DC-DC conversion require a

redesign of the internal harness to high-voltage specifications, which we believe
positions ACES to ship a growing volume of high-voltage DLO cables over time.

strong contribution from two major ASIC customers. Looking ahead, with the new

Broadening connector footprint in ASICs
Since Nvidia has strict control over its supply chain, ACES previously supplied only
the daughterboard high-speed connectors for GB200/300 servers, at roughly 10 to
20 units per rack. On ASIC platforms, since ODMs have greater influence over
supplier selection, ACES secures share on both the motherboard and the
daughterboard, lifting content to around 30 units per rack.

Besides, ACES historically supplied only MCIO connectors to these ASIC customers,
but as further qualifications clear, the company will broaden its connector product
offerings in 2026 to include Multi-Track, board-to-board, and E1/E3 connectors.
We expect the contribution from ASIC customers to scale further in 2H26, driving
server revenue to grow 64% HoH.

Riding the Gen6 ASP curve with a compatibility advantage
Aside from the spectacular growth in the connector shipments, we expect ACES to
benefit from the ongoing Gen6 connector upgrade cycle, with ASPs expected to
increase by 15%. The AI server trays currently adopt a mix of Gen5 and Gen6
connectors, but we expect the penetration of Gen6 connectors to gradually
increase going forward as the migration from Gen5 to Gen6 is the first major

generational shift since Genoa introduced Gen5 in 2022.

Figure 15
Gen6 connector ASP is 15% higher than Gen5
Source: SinoPac
Only three to four connector vendors are qualified by CSPs at Gen6, and ACES is
one of them. Furthermore, ACES can supply both Gen5 and Gen6 platforms under
its existing qualification, with no need to re-sample or re-qualify for Gen5. This is
because Gen6 connectors keep footprint backward compatibility with Gen5. By
contrast, other peers must re-sample and run through the full qualification process

again to move up to Gen6.
About the PCIe migration and Gen6 spec upgrade
PCIe has long been used for on-board and in-chassis interconnects. In the Vera
Rubin platform, PCIe Gen6 serves as the in-tray bridge to front-of-tray modules
such as CX-9 SuperNICs, BlueField-4 DPUs and NVMe storage. GPU-to-GPU scale-
up runs on NVLink, with UALink as the open alternative; these fabrics work
alongside PCIe.
In order to address ever-larger training models, the PCIe must scale with the fast-

growing size of model parameters and datasets. The move from Gen5 to Gen6 is
not a simple speed doubling, even though per-lane bandwidth rises from 32 GT/s
to 64 GT/s. It reworks the link from the coding scheme up. Gen1 through Gen5
doubled I/O speed using NRZ signaling. Gen6 switches to PAM4 four-level
signaling and, for the first time, adds FEC (Forward Error Correction), which lets the
receiver detect and correct transmission errors on its own.

Each generation doubles bandwidth while tightening signal-integrity specs, which
demands higher-precision connector manufacturing. This creates a real barrier to
entry for high-speed connector suppliers.

ACES’s networking product portfolio includes set-top boxes, WiFi devices, routers,

Automotive: rising electronic content

Industrial: a steady UPS-driven base
ACES supplies industrial power cords, used mainly in uninterruptible power systems
(UPS). A UPS is an electrical device that acts as a reliable buffer when the main
power supply behaves abnormally, such as during a fault, outage, or unstable
voltage. It keeps automation controllers, sensors, and actuators continuously

powered so equipment keeps running. When a power problem occurs, the UPS
supplies stable backup power to prevent equipment failure, data corruption, or
system crashes.  We expect ACES’s industrial business to grow steadily, with growth
of 9% and 14% in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Figure 29 Figure 30
ACES Industrial product portfolio Industrial sales and YoY
Source: ACES Source: SinoPac

easing component shortages and broader AI adoption, which should drive consumer
replacement demand, lifting global NB shipments -5%/5% year over year in
2027/28, which is a gradual recovery following a weaker 2026.

Power rack: leveraging its long-standing relationship with Schneider

ACES has a long-standing relationship with Schneider Electric at its Philippine plant.
The partnership dates back more than 20 years, with ACES’s Philippine plant
initially serving Schneider almost exclusively. Over the years, the product portfolio
has expanded from conventional power cables to uninterruptible power systems
(UPS) and, more recently, power racks for AI datacentres.
ACES began shipping high-voltage power cables for power racks in 1Q26 and

entered the volume ramp-up phase in 2Q26. We remain positive on power rack
adoption as the PSU in every IT tray hits a physical limit as rack power reaches
1MW, occupies rack space and caps compute density. Moving power shelves and
BBU/CBU into a dedicated power rack decouples power from computing and frees

Initiate with an Outperform rating
We initiate coverage of ACES with an Outperform rating given its dual growth
engine from Bloom’s strong expansion and high-speed connector customer wins
and generation upgrades. We forecast 2027/28 EPS of NT$11.19/NT$14.93, with
our 2027 forecast 48% ahead of consensus. Our TP of NT$270 is based on 20x
2H27-1H28SP PE, backed by a 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP driven by Bloom’s

significant ramp and larger content in server products. Initiate coverage with an
Outperform rating.
Company profile
ACES was founded in 1996, and it mainly produces connectors, micro-coaxial cable,
high-speed transmission cables, and RF connectors. Connectors and cable
assemblies link two separate subsystems or operating units and carry the signals
between them, allowing the overall system to function properly. They are used
across a wide range of electronic products, with main applications spanning

notebooks and PC peripherals, consumer electronics, automotive electronics, cloud
servers, networking equipment, and industrial equipment.
To keep pace with the push towards lighter, thinner, and smaller notebooks and
consumer devices, ACES developed high-temperature-resistant, fine-pitch, and
low-profile products at an early stage. At the same time, it is moving actively into
the high-frequency, high-speed connectors, high-speed cables, and power supplies

required for cloud servers, as well as the connector and cable assemblies used in
automotive electronics.

Company development
ACES has used M&A well to gain new capabilities, plants and technology, which in
turn opened doors to new customers and markets. In 2015, it bought MEC IMEX
to add cable assembly capability and a plant in the Philippines, which took it into
the industrial market. In 2017, the CPC deal gave it access to the European auto
connector market and a plant in Vietnam. In 2019, it bought Kuang Ying to add

metal stamping capacity and reach Taiwan telecom customers. The key deal came
in 2021, when ACES bought 100% of Genesis, a US high-speed interconnect maker
based in Atlanta. The main goal was to gain Genesis’s long-standing ties with US
CSP and cloud customers. In 2025, ACES opened its Phase Two R&D headquarters
in Taoyuan, which focuses on cloud connectors and high-speed cables. We believe
10 years of M&A have turned ACES from a consumer connector maker into a

supplier of high-speed interconnect solutions for AI servers and the cloud.

Cashflow and balance sheet
ACES’s balance sheet shows healthy debt exposure with an improving net cash
level. Given its plan to massively expand capacity at its Taiwan plants, we expect
Capex to remain at a high level of NT$1,075m in 2026 and then slightly decline to
NT$855m in 2027. We forecast its depreciation-to-sales ratio to be around 3% in

2027, which is at the low end of its historical level.
We expect ACES’s ROE to expand from 10% in 2026 to 21% in 2027 and 23% in
2028, as the volume ramp of AI high-speed connectors and Bloom Energy power
cables drives a richer product mix and gross margin expansion, delivering 121% EPS
growth in 2027.
We expect ACES’s FCF to turn positive in 2026 as operating cashflow scales with
the AI high-speed connector and Bloom power cable ramp while Capex normalises

after the 2026 capacity buildout. Looking ahead, we expect ACES’s free cashflow
per share to be NT$0.8-1.5 in coming years.

Competitive positioning

In high-speed connectors, ACES competes primarily with overseas peers including
Amphenol (APH), TE, and Molex. APH’s edge lies in a development-technology lead of
one to two generations, while ACES’s relative strength rests on customers’ recognition
on delivering quality comparable to the overseas majors, alongside more flexible lead
times, strong capacity-expansion support, and fast ramp-up.
The structural opportunity comes from the supply chain integration trend. As

transmission speeds rise, CSPs increasingly prefer suppliers which can integrate high-
speed connectors with high-speed cables and ship assembled, finished goods.

### Page 29

Wired for growth ACES - O-PF
6 October 2026 29
ACES is one of the few Taiwanese players with this vertical-integration footprint: it
holds wire-drawing technology transferred in-house from ITRI, produces everything
from wire drawing through assembly on its own, and its commitment to moving into
high-speed cables has been noted by AWS and Meta, backed by roughly two years of
qualification work already completed.
By contrast, other Taiwanese peers have yet to build comparable vertical integration.

Customers have historically split connectors and cables across separate suppliers, with
cable suppliers represented by BizLink (3665 TT) and Longwell (6290 TT), while peers
such as Lotes (3533 TT), Alltop (3526 TT), Argosy (3217 TT) and JPC Connectivity
(6197 TT) are likewise strong in connectors but lack in-house cable capability.
Since the Gen5 qualified-supplier base was already fragmented, the Gen6 bar of
combined connector-plus-cable capability becomes ACES’s entry point, bringing ACES

into design-in from Gen6. The qualified connector supplier base is small, with suppliers
including APH, TE, and ACES, with Lotes added subsequently.
Peer comparison
ACES’s primary local competitors in connectors include Lotes, Alltop, Argosy and JPC
Connectivity. In the cable industry, it competes with Bizlink and Longwell. ACES’s
foreign peers include Amphenol, TE and FIT.

Target price calculation
We initiate coverage of ACES with an O-PF rating and a target price of NT$270,
based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, supported by a strong 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-

28SP. We use a PE-based methodology for ACES, in line with other connector
companies. We initiate coverage with an Outperform rating.
Our target multiple of 20x is in line with the 2027SP average PE of 20.4x for Taiwan-
listed connector and cable peers with direct AI datacentre exposure, namely
BizLink, Lotes and JPC Connectivity. We view this as a reasonable anchor. ACES
offers faster earnings growth than the group, with 2027SP EPS growth of 121%

versus the peer average of 52%, while its 2027-28SP ROE of 21-24% sits at the low
end of the peer range of 22-52%.

Wired for growth ACES - O-PF

We also believe the target PE premium is justified as the AI revenue contribution,
including AI data transmission and power, rises from 4% in 2024 to 48% in 2027SP.
The company maintains its key supplier position at Bloom as the company ramps up
its capacity expansion, while ASIC customer wins and higher connector content
from the Gen6 upgrade cycle should provide additional growth drivers. As the
company gradually shifts away from its legacy consumer electronics base and its

exposure to cyclical downturns, we see room for a structural rerating.

Key risks
We see four key operational risks for ACES:
Raw material cost volatility: Copper prices drive a large share of Cogs for ACES's
cable products, while connectors carry far less exposure. ACES currently reprices
its cable products with customers on a quarterly basis, which has allowed it to

sustain a long-term GM of around 20% on power cables. However, the pass-through
might be lagging, so sharp copper price swings could cause quarterly GM to
fluctuate before repricing impact catches up.
Delays in ODM pull-in: ACES ships its high-speed connectors directly to ODM
customers. Because rack assembly sits at the final stage of the AI server supply
chain, any upstream disruption, such as components shortages and assembly yield
issue, could push out rack shipment schedules or reduce volumes. This could in turn

lead ODMs to cut or defer orders for high-speed connectors from ACES, creating
quarter-to-quarter volatility in server revenue.
Slower-than-expected ramp-up at Bloom Energy: Power cables for Bloom will
account for 28%/35% of ACES's revenue in 2027/28SP, which makes Bloom's
ramp-up the single largest swing factor in our forecasts. Delays could arise on both
the supply and demand sides. On the supply side, Bloom could fall behind its
capacity plan if new facility fit-out, equipment installation or hiring takes longer

than expected. On the demand side, its datacentre customers could defer
deployments due to grid interconnection, gas supply or financing hurdles, or due to
regulatory headwinds such as tighter permitting and emissions rules for on-site gas-
fired generation, utility restrictions on behind-the-meter power, or changes to fuel
cell tax incentives. Either scenario would directly reduce ACES's power cable
shipments. As volumes scale, Bloom may also qualify a second cable supplier, which

would add pressure to ACES's share and ASP.
Prolonged NB weakness: NB and Consumer remains ACES's largest segment at 35%
of 2026SP revenue, and our forecasts assume it returns to 4%/12% growth in
2027/28SP on a Windows 12-driven replacement cycle. If end-demand stays soft
or the replacement cycle slips, a further NB decline could offset part of the growth
from Bloom and AI servers and dilute group GM.

### Page 31

Wired for growth ACES - O-PF
6 October 2026 31
Investment thesis
ACES is evolving from a notebook connector maker into a supplier of AI
interconnects and power cables, with two growth engines ramping at the same
time. The first is Bloom Energy. ACES has co-developed SOFC power cables with
Bloom for about six years, and it is currently one of three suppliers of Bloom's power
cable assemblies, with around one-third of the total. It ships inner PCS and hot-box

cables in 2026 and adds external DLO high-voltage cables from 2027, with order
visibility extending to 2028 and beyond. The second engine is AI/ASIC servers. On
ASIC platforms, ODMs have more say in supplier selection, so ACES wins both
motherboard and daughterboard sockets, which lifts content from 10-20 to ~30
units per rack. Gen6 raises connector ASP by ~15%, and a new in-chassis PCIe Gen6
cable line, qualified by two CSPs, adds a higher-content stream. ACES integrates

vertically from wire drawing to final assembly, which makes it one of the few
Taiwanese suppliers able to ship connector-plus-cable solutions as CSPs
consolidate their vendor base.
Catalysts
(1) Bloom's capacity ramp from 2GW to 4GW/6GW in 2026-28 and new data-
center wins (Oracle, Nebius, AEP, Equinix) converting into cable orders; (2) DLO
high-voltage cable shipments starting in 2027; (3) ASIC customer ramps in 2H26
and a broader product range beyond MCIO into Multi-Track, board-to-board and

E1/E3 connectors; (4) rising Gen6 penetration on AI server trays; (5) high-speed
cable lines expanding from two to eight by end-2026, with GM improving from
~20% toward ~30%.
Valuation details
We adopt a PE-based valuation methodology as we believe the market will mainly
focus on the earnings growth outlooks of high-speed connector makers, which is
better reflected with PE metrics. We assign a target multiple of 20x PE, more than
+1sd of the three-year average, to reflect a strong outlook with margin expansion

and an EPS Cagr of 72% in 2026-28 on solid Bloom Energy demand and increasing
high speed connector content.
Investment risks
There could be downside risk to sales growth and margins if: (1) Bloom's
deployments or backlog conversion slow on financing, gas supply or permitting
delays, or ACES loses share among Bloom's cable suppliers; (2) ASIC programs ramp
more slowly or CSP Capex is cut; (3) Gen6 adoption is slower than expected, or
competition from Amphenol, TE, Molex and Lotes pressures ASP; (4) high-speed

cable yield and GM improve more slowly than we assume; (5) NB and consumer
demand weakens further on component shortages, tariffs and inflation; (6) TWD
appreciation or higher copper prices squeeze margins.

## Document 275: GFHK - MediaTek update

### Page 1

100% EMIB-T, 100% Market Share in v9  
C Progress Intact, with >$170 Annualized EPS 
Potential

Maintained  Buy 
 Target price NT$ 6,650

Street-high TPU estimates and up TP to NT$6,650: MTK fell 7% yesterday on market noise 
around a potential v8t delay, insufficient EMIB supply, and share‑loss concerns in v9. We hold 
an opposite view, as our research indicates the v8t/Zebrafish remains intact, demand is well 
above the 2027 CoWoS allocation of 180k, EMIB ‑T/substrate yields and supply are tracking 
better than expected, and MTK retains 100% share in the v9/2nm. We remain constructive, 
as MTK’s datacenter business is well-positioned for share gains in TPU across volume, value 
and workloads. Beyond TPU, we expect improving visibility on the 2nd ASIC project, likely 
SpaceX ASIC (our report: Likely SpaceX Win on May 27) instead of AI6, in 4Q26. We revised 
our earnings estimates by 0%/+3% in 2026E/2027E and accordingly lift TP from NT$6,420 to 
NT$6,650. We now forecast its datacenter ASIC to be NT$2.8bn/$18.6bn/NT$75.7bn.  
Market noise around adding CoWoS on top of EMIB ‑T makes little sense : Contrary to 
recent sell‑side reports suggesting CoWoS as a way to improve v9 supply, we continue to 
believe v9, both Humufish and Triggerfish, will be 100% on EMIB‑T, with life‑cycle volume of 
>5m (vs bears at >2m). We reiterate our view ( Intel: Yield Expansion  on Sept 15) that two 
major suppliers have improved yields from 20–25% on initial testing vehicles in June to ~45% 
on the August vehicles and ~50% currently. This is on track to reach the >50% yield target in 
1Q27. Supported by three substrate suppliers with dedicated lines (incl. Gama fab), we expect 
small‑volume substrate deliveries in mid‑2027 of ~100k and mass‑volume by 4Q27.  
Market noise around MTK’s share loss in v9 makes little sense: Contrary to worries over 
MTK’s share loss in v9, we expect MTK to gain 100% share in v9, with Humufish for training 
and Triggerfish for inferencing, while believe that there is no other 2nm project during the time 
frame. Near term, the key milestone to watch is the engineering tape‑out in Nov, and as noted, 
we believe the passing bar is not high and believe the current chipset testing at TSMC for 
both compute tile and Serdes is largely on track. For v10, we still expect MTK to obtain the 
major SKU, Icefish, while AMD+MRVL or AVGO is competing with the other SKU, Merope.  
Market noise around MTK’s v8t/Zebrafish overly bearish: Contrary to worries over MTK’s 
v8t/Zebrafish delay to 2Q27, we still expect the SKU to start shipments in 4Q26, backed by 
mgmt’s guidance of >$2bn ASIC revenue in 2026. We believe the demand is much higher 
than the 2027’s CoWoS allocation of 180k and expect the life cycle to be well extended.  
Risks: (1) AI demand slowdown; (2) consumer demand; (3) peer competition.

Jeff Pu, CFA

### Page 5

Company Report | MediaTek 
5

Valuation and Recommendation  
Maintain Buy with TP of $6,650 
We rate MTK (2454 TT) Buy and set our TP at NT$ 6,650 which is based on 45x our 2027E EPS 
estimate or 18x 2028E EPS. We raise our 2027 multiple to reflect higher earnings growth in 2027/2028, 
but it suggests an undemanding valuation for 2028. MTK’s P/E traded at an average of 18x since 2021 
but was up to an average of 2 2x since 1Q24 when the company starts to see the AI op portunity. Our 
target multiple for 2027 is much higher than the mid-cycle level given Google TPU momentum and our 
bullish estimate of its 2027E/2028E earnings growth.  
We adopt 2027 and/or 2028 as valuation basis to capture its datacenter ASIC potential which is the key 
market focus  and expect the business to take off starting 2027.  We adopt P/E methodology as we 
believe the market now mainly focuses on the company’s earnings outlook, and we usually apply P/E 
method to downstream firms.  
Fundamentally, w e are positive on MTK, as it is well -positioned to expand its value position in 
smartphone SoCs amid the edge -AI trend, as well as the expansion into datacenter/enterprise ASICs 
and smart automotive solutions. The datacenter ASIC business is the k ey for upside, in our view, and 
we expect TPU to provide a volume boost for MTK, while the market will continue to monitor for its 
execution.

## Document 279: GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-04
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild
Date:    10 hours ago
GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKET CALL: Bond
Vigilantes Gone Wild
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 4 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Jené Stephaniuk / Unsplash
The Bond Vigilantes have gone wild worldwide, pushing government bond
yields higher in developed and emerging markets alike.
A month ago, we asked whether rising yields reflected stronger growth,
higher inflation, or looming fiscal crises. We still think the answer is mostly
growth. The exception is where government finances are weakest. There,
bond investors are charging a fiscal-risk premium. France may be on the

verge of a full-blown debt crisis.
Here's more:

### Page 3

I. Global Bond Yields
US Treasury yields rose on Friday even though September payrolls
increased only 29,000. The 10-year US Treasury yield ended the week at
5.28%, after hitting its highest level since 2002 midweek (chart). Higher
real yields account for nearly all of this year's increase, while inflation
expectations have barely budged. In the UK and Australia, 10-year yields
are higher still, at 5.37% and 5.36%. Japan's is 3.09% and still climbing.

The French government now pays more than Italy or Greece to borrow for
10 years (chart). The yield spread between the French and the German
10-year government bonds jumped on Thursday, when France unveiled a
2027 budget that freezes public-sector pay and most pensions to limit the
budget deficit to 5% of GDP. The spread is now the widest since the
Eurozone debt crisis of 2012. German yields fell last week as French
yields rose. Inflation is running hot in both countries, so the gap reflects

France's finances.

### Page 4

Six of the 22 bond markets on our list have seen 10-year yields climb
100bps or more this year (chart). France leads at 131bps, with the US
second at 112bps. Italy, Indonesia, Japan, and South Korea round out the
group.
Two-year government note yields are above central banks' official policy
rates in all six economies on our chart, by as much as 107bps in the UK
(chart). The gap is smallest in Australia, at 34bps. The Reserve Bank of
Australia raised its cash rate by 25bps to 4.60% on September 29, its

fourth hike this year.

### Page 5

II. Global Stocks
South Korea's ETF has nearly doubled this year, up 97.4% in dollars, with
Taiwan's up 83.1% (chart). The Emerging Markets ex-China ETF (EMXC)
is up 39.4%, and its lead over the broader Emerging Markets ETF (EEM)
widened to 15.7 percentage points last week from 14.9. We continue to
prefer EMXC over EEM. France's ETF is down 6.1%, the weakest
developed market on our list. The US ETF (SPY) is up 12.9%, in the top
half of the rankings.

### Page 6

Stay Home vs Go Global match has been in a stalemate in recent months.
The US ETF trails the All Country World ex-US ETF (ACWX) by only 0.3
points ytd, versus 1.0 a week earlier. The MSCI USA ETF (PBUS) and
ACWX have moved in lockstep, and both pulled back from record highs
last week (chart).
III. Currencies
The dollar has rebounded off the bottom of its long-term uptrend channel.
The US Dollar Index (DXY) is at 101.9, up from 99.2 at the start of

September (chart). We have expected the dollar to stay firm, and it has.
The euro's slide accounts for much of the dollar's gain, since the euro
carries a 57.6% weight in the DXY. The euro is trading at $1.13, its lowest

### Page 7

since May 2025 (chart). It fell sharply on Thursday, alongside French
bonds.
The yen is trading at 157.68 per dollar, near the middle of its 153-160
range since the joint US-Japan intervention in late July (chart). Japan's
latest data show no intervention since late August.
IV. Weekly Focus
(1) Seaborne crude oil shipments exiting the Gulf have rebounded to
roughly 16.5-17.5 million barrels per day (averaging around 98% of pre-
war levels, excluding Iran). Major producers like Saudi Arabia have driven

much of this rebound. While crude flows have recovered, refined fuels like
diesel face severe ongoing bottlenecks. Refined product flows remain a
fraction of pre-war norms (hovering well below historical averages),

### Page 8

keeping global diesel supplies and pricing tight. On Friday, the G7
announced the coordinated release of up to 100 million barrels of
emergency oil and diesel inventories.
(2) Inflation data across Europe underscored lingering stickiness, with
annual prints registering at 3.3% in Germany, 3.0% in France, 4.1% in
Italy, and 4.9% in Spain.
(3) In Asia, a hotter-than-expected Tokyo-area inflation report reinforced
expectations of further policy normalization by the Bank of Japan (BOJ).

Tokyo’s core CPI (excluding fresh food) jumped to 2.7% y/y in September,
accelerating sharply from 1.8% in August and pushing inflation back
above the BOJ’s 2.0% target for the first time in several months (chart).
The core-core measure climbed even higher, to 3.0% (up from 2.0% in
August). Services inflation also rose notably, to 2.3%.

## Document 402: 【國泰證期研究部】4Q26 PCB產業展望-供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚-20260930

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】4Q26 PCB產業展望-供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚-20260930.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源

### Page 1

1

4Q26 PCB 產業展望 
供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚

### Page 2

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/09/30

2 
4Q26 PCB 產業展望 
供需失衡、銅價飆漲，銅箔價格續揚 
結論與建議 
1. 銅箔過往主要以 HVLP1/2等級為主，而在更低的傳輸損耗/延遲要求下，進入 26年，多
數平台包括 AI伺服器及交換器，在銅箔的選用上已升級至 HVLP3，且部分終端已將規格
提升至 HVLP4，包含 NVIDIA Vera Rubin伺服器平台、AMD新平台 MI400系列；ASIC
方面，26年以 AWS T 3為大宗，以及 1.6T高速交換機同樣採用到 HVLP4銅箔進行搭配。 
2. 考量下一世代 AI 產品將逐步出貨，研究部預估整體 HVLP4 需求將在 4Q26 超過 1,000
噸/月(以 Hoz 計算)，儘管銅箔龍頭供應商三井持續宣布擴充產能，台廠金居也宣布增加
產能並持續將低階銅箔產能轉至 HVLP，再加上終端逐步認證中國供應商供給，但預期以
現階段的產能仍無法滿足未來幾年持續增加的需求。 
3. 考量 4Q26高階 HVLP產品需求大幅提升，銅箔廠商將持續將產能轉向高階需求，研究部
認為產品組合轉佳力度將由於銅價侵蝕之影響，整體來看，銅箔廠商將有望受惠於由銅價
走升所帶動之營收成長，以及產能逐步由低階轉向高階占比，帶動毛利率持續轉好之趨勢。

4Q26 投資展望 
1. 研究部認為銅箔產業漲價趨勢將持續，且漲價邏輯已由單純銅價成本轉嫁，逐步轉向高階
HVLP 產品供需緊俏所帶動的加工費提升。隨高階銅箔 HVLP3/4 需求自 2H26 因下一世
代 AI 產品需求增加下加速，而新增產能仍受到良率、認證及產能轉換限制，預期高階銅
箔價格將由成本轉嫁逐步轉向稀缺性定價，4Q26 供應商議價能力仍有進一步提升空間，
帶動營收與獲利表現上行，主要受惠台廠為金居(8358 TT)。 
2.  AI PCB受惠於 NV GPU AI Server 以及各大 CSP的 ASIC Server 升級迭代需求，同時也
一併帶動 CCL 材料規格朝向升級趨勢不變，新世代產品規格皆往高階原物料靠攏，且包
含玻纖布、銅箔在內等重要原料價格持續高漲，有益於 CCL 廠商持續執行漲價策略，故
維持看好 CCL廠商後市表現，包含台光電(2383 TT)、台燿(6274 TT)、聯茂(6213 TT)。

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/09/30

3 
高階 CCL 規格提升，銅箔供需失衡延續 
AI PCB受惠於 NV GPU AI Server 以及各大 CSP的 ASIC Server升級迭代需求，包括應用於 GPU、
ASIC AI Server 及 800G/1.6T的交換器板等高階 PCB需求，於近兩年產值大幅提升。同時也一併
帶動 CCL材料規格朝向升級趨勢不變，新世代產品規格皆往高階原物料靠攏。 
CCL 三大上游原材料為樹脂、銅箔、玻纖布。26 年初以來，我們看到玻纖布市場因主要廠商向高
規格產品靠攏降低產能下，產品價格飆漲趨勢至年底仍尚未停止，而高階產品也在需求大幅開出之
下亦將面臨短缺問題。而在銅箔方面，過往主要以 HVLP1/2等級為主，而在更低的傳輸損耗/延遲
要求下，進入 26 年，多數平台包括 AI 伺服器及交換器，在銅箔的選用上已升級至 HVLP3，並且
有部分終端已將規格提升至 HVLP4，帶動銅箔廠商營收增長，包含 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 伺服器平
台中 CCL需求主要 Compute Tray、Switch Tray、Midplane上，其中 CCL規格包括 M8~M9(含 
M8+)，AMD新平台 MI400系列也亦將採用 M8搭配 HVLP4等級規格。而 AI ASIC 平台方面，
美系 CSP 持續提高資本支出預算，同時自主研發 ASIC 晶片趨勢愈發激烈，26年主要以 AWS的 
Trainum 3為採用 HVLP4為大宗，而我們觀察到 Google TPU V8 則仍以 HVLP3配合 M8之 CCL
規格，預期將要於下一代 V9 才會升級至 HVLP4。另外，為追求極致的傳輸損耗，1.6T 高速交換
機同樣採用到 HVLP4銅箔進行搭配。 
圖一：各 HVLP 規格銅箔示意圖                                                                                  圖二：銅箔基板 CCL 
資料來源：三井金屬、國泰證期研究部整 
表一：各家 AI 晶片平台採用 HVLP4 方案規格

### Page 4

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/09/30

4 
過往材料主要供應商掌握在大部分日商手中，包含三井金屬、古河、福田以及歐洲盧森堡，然而多
數廠商在擴廠方面皆較為保守，導致高階原料出現供不應求。隨著更多的高階 AI 平台採用高階銅
箔，我們觀察到銅箔市場緊缺程度逐漸在擴大。因考量銅箔規格由 HVLP1/2提升到 HVLP3/4，產
能約略將下降一半，加上現階段 AI用高階銅箔皆以 Hoz為主，甚至未來採行至 Toz，將近一步降
低銅箔廠商有效產能。考量下一世代 AI產品將逐步出貨，研究部預估整體 HVLP4需求將在 4Q26
超過 1,000 噸/月(以 Hoz 計算)，儘管銅箔龍頭供應商三井持續宣布擴充產能，台廠金居也宣布增
加產能並持續將低階銅箔產能轉至 HVLP，再加上終端逐步認證中國供應商供給，但預期以現階段
的產能仍無法滿足未來幾年持續增加的需求。 
圖三：HVLP4 供需預期(以 Hoz 計算) 
資料來源：國泰證期研究部整 
銅現貨價持續上漲，有助銅箔廠持續轉嫁成本 
銅箔廠商購買銅線浸泡於硫酸溶液中做離子交換，再用滾筒製造銅箔。對銅箔廠而言，公司每月以
平均銅價加上加工費向下游 CCL 廠商進行報價，因此銅價格與其營收以及利潤結構息息相關。觀
察今年銅價整體維持高檔震盪並較去年明顯上行，LME銅價於年初 US$11,000/噸，一路升至目前
9M26 US$14,000/噸以上的水準，年內漲幅約接近 30%，主要受到礦端供給受限、冶煉原料偏緊，
以及資料中心、電網與能源轉型相關需求支撐。對銅箔廠而言，銅價上漲趨動產品 ASP 與帳面營
收成長，即使實際出貨量不變，營收金額仍可能隨原料銅價上升而增加。考量到銅礦供給持續受限，
以及美國恐對精煉銅進口加徵關稅之下，全球銅庫存持續下探，4Q26 銅價格亦有望持續向上攀升。 
另外，儘管銅箔廠商向下游報價採用前一個月之平均銅價格，在銅價快速走升的情況下，有時無法
及時跟進價格調整，導致毛利受到部分侵蝕，惟考量 4Q26高階 HVLP產品需求大幅提升，銅箔廠
商將持續將產能轉向高階需求，研究部認為產品組合轉佳力度將由於銅價侵蝕之影響，整體來看，
銅箔廠商將有望受惠於由銅價走升所帶動之營收成長，以及產能逐步由低階轉向高階占比，帶動毛
利率持續轉好之趨勢。

仍有進一步提升空間，帶動營收與獲利表現上行。

智邦 2345 TT  ( 電子–通信網路 )

不畏風雨、小蹲高躍 
投資與評價 
1. 智邦 2Q26 在 800G 產品強勁需求推動下，營運優於預期；因 AI 加速器與
高階網路交換器拉貨需求仍處產業旺季，3Q26 營運續創新高。 
2. 受惠資料中心高階產品規格升級趨勢，800G 網路交換器需求成長相隨，AI
應用增加美系客戶佈建更多高階運算加速器 ， 且網路交換器升級加快 ， 智邦
26 年 EPS 85.18 元與 27 年預估 EPS 107.53 元，營運續創歷史新高。 
3. 目前智邦股價 1,895.0 元，約為 17.6X 27(F)PER ，低於近三年參考本益比
區間 14~35X 中間偏下位置，考量資料中心需求快速成長與高階產品滲透
增加，26 年與 27 年營運可望接續創高，維持買進投資評等，由於營運旺
季將過，且受零組件漲價影響與 AI 加速卡產品營收快速增加，使 2H26 毛
利率略低於 1H26 水準，故將本益比評價略微調降至中間偏下約 22 倍位
置，目標價 2,380 元(22X 修正後 27(F)PER)。 
重點更新 
智邦 2Q26 進入產業旺季，營運為歷史單季新高 
智邦因進入傳統旺季與工作天數回升，資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路
交換器產品，再加上新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於季中正式量產，自 4 月與 5 月營
收皆創新高，6 月營收更是大幅躍升，2Q26 整體營收為 955.38 億元(+36.2% 
QoQ)，高於預估 7.1%，因高階網路交換器佔比自 1Q26 起已提高，毛利率
19.7%略高於上季， 由於新產品開發與產能擴充所需，營業費用 44.91 億元
(+23.0% QoQ)，略高於預估，營業淨利仍可季增 42.6%至 143.31 億元，優於
預期，稅後淨利 110.53 億元(+32.5% QoQ)，EPS 19.78 元，營運為歷史新高。 
預期智邦 3Q26 仍處產業旺季，營運將再創新高 
智邦 3Q26 仍處傳統旺季，美系資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路交換器
產品，新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於本季完整放量出貨，7 月營收雖與 6 月持平，
仍為歷史次高水準，8 月營收再創歷史新高，首次站上四百億元大關，預期美系
客戶對於 AI 加速卡需求依然強勁，故上修整體營收預估 61.4%至 1,219.21 億
元(+27.6% QoQ)。受產品組合轉變與部份零組件漲價影響，略微調低毛利率預
估至 18.1%，但也因營收規模擴張，使營業費用率下降至 3.9%，故調升營業淨
利預估 62.4%至 173.36 億元(+21.0% QoQ)，稅後淨利 133.65 億元(+20.9% 
QoQ)，EPS 23.91 元，營運再創新高。

智邦 2Q26 進入產業旺季，營運創歷史單季新高 
智邦因進入傳統旺季與工作天數回升 ， 資料中心客戶加速拉貨800G 高階網路交換器產品 ， 再加
上新一代 AI 加速卡產品也於季中正式量產，自4 月營收 273.60 億元(+9.4% MoM)與 5 月營收
286.23 億元(+4.6% MoM)皆續創新高，6 月營收 395.55 億元(+38.2% MoM)更是大幅躍升、
維持成長趨勢不變，2Q26 整體營收為 955.38 億元(+36.2% QoQ)，高於預估之 892.36 億元
(+27.3% QoQ) 7.1%，因高階網路交換器佔比自 1Q26 起已提高，毛利率 19.7%略高於上季之
19.5%，但新產品開發與產能擴充所需，營業費用 44.91 億元(+23.0% QoQ)，使營業費用率為
4.7%，略高於預估之 4.5%，營業淨利 143.31 億元(+42.6% QoQ)，高於預估之 136.29 億元
(+35.6% QoQ) 5.1%，稅後淨利 110.53 億元(+32.5% QoQ)，EPS 19.78 元，營運為歷史新高。

預期智邦 3Q26 仍處產業旺季，營運續創新高 
智邦 3Q26 仍處傳統旺季，美系資料中心客戶加速拉貨 800G 高階網路交換器產品，新一代 AI
加速卡產品也於本季完整放量出貨，7 月營收 394.73 億元(-0.2% MoM)雖與 6 月持平，仍為歷
史次高水準，8 月營收 408.73 億元(+3.5%MoM、+59.4% YoY)，再創歷史新高，也為首次站
上四百億元大關，較去年同期大幅成長，預期在美系客戶對於 AI 加速卡需求相當強勁之下，9
月營收則續創新高，故上修整體營收預估 61.4%至 1,219.21 億元(+27.6% QoQ) 。雖受產品組
合改變與部份零組件漲價影響，略微調低毛利率預估至18.1%，但也因營收規模擴張，使營業費
用率由上季之 4.7%下降至 3.9%，調升營業淨利預估 62.4%至 173.36 億元(+21.0% QoQ)，稅
後淨利 133.65 億元(+20.9% QoQ)，EPS 23.91 元，營運續創歷史新高。

預期智邦 26 年因 AI 加速器與網路交換器持續升級，獲利創新高 
自 25 年初 CSP 業者加速佈建自家開發 ASIC 產品，並導入 AI 伺服器應用中，接續在年底推出
新版本，預期於2Q26 底放量出貨，日趨完整的白牌生態系可節省同等算力三成以上支出，可控
的產品發展進度降低了對於單一品牌業者的依賴。從ARM 架構 CPU、訓練用加速器、智慧網卡
與高階網路交換器共同構成美系客戶在雲端算力服務的高性價比競爭力，可推廣至更多中小企
業與價格敏感度高的潛在客戶 ， 期望在初期透過生態系的高黏著度 ， 使其成為該美系客戶的長期
合作夥伴。智邦因為美系客戶用於資料中心之 AI 運算加速卡與企業用高階網路交換器產品需求
強勁，受惠運算加速器逐代推出與美系客戶800G 網路交換器提前於 1H26 加速拉貨，使2Q26
定單需求強勁優於預期 ， 故上調智邦26 年整體營收預估 13.7%至 4,177.15 億元(+68.2% YoY)，
毛利率雖受 2H26 零組件大幅漲價影響與產品組合變化影響，略微調低毛利率預估至 18.8%，
仍較 25 年之 18.1%回升，營業淨利年增 88.3%至 604.98 億元，稅後淨利預估為 476.04 億元
(+80.7% YoY)，EPS 85.18 元，獲利為歷史新高。 
智邦 27 年受惠 AI 加速器與 800G 網路交換器需求成長，預期營運再創新高 
智邦 27 年因美系雲端業者持續擴大 AI 伺服器建置規模，又有新世代產品接替，預期於 2Q27
量產，同時 800G 網路交換器需求暢旺，再加上 2H27 可望小量產出 1.6T 產品，除此外，企業
客戶亦開始加速導入 800G，電信商需求也已持穩下，故上修整體營收預估 17.0%至 5,269.97
億元(+26.2% YoY)，即使毛利率較高之 800G 以上產品需求亦強勁，但因有 AI 加速器產品快速
增加，使整體毛利率將降至 18.4%，由於營收規模擴增，營業費用率也隨之下降至 4.0%，調高
營業淨利預估 9.4%至 758.57 億元(+25.4% YoY)，稅後淨利 600.96 億元(+26.2% YoY)，EPS 
107.53 元，再創新高。

### Page 4

AI 應用推動白牌生態系擴張，營運快速增長 
智邦 2Q26 在 800G 產品強勁需求推動下，營運優於預期；智邦因 AI 加速器與高階網路交換器
拉貨需求仍處產業旺季，3Q26 營運將創新高。受惠資料中心高階產品規格升級趨勢，800G 網
路交換器需求成長相隨，AI 應用增加美系客戶佈建更多高階運算加速器，且網路交換器升級加
快，智邦 26 年 EPS 85.18 元與 27 年預估 EPS 107.53 元，營運續創歷史新高。 
目前智邦股價 1,895.0 元，約為 17.6X 27(F)PER ，低於近三年參考本益比區間 14~35X 中間偏
下位置，考量資料中心需求快速成長與高階產品滲透增加，26 年與 27 年營運可望接續創高，
維持買進投資評等，由於營運旺季將過，且受零組件漲價影響與 AI 加速卡產品營收快速增加，
使 2H26 毛利率略低於 1H26 水準，故將本益比評價由中間偏上之30 倍略微調降至中間偏下約
22 倍位置 ， 目標價由3,000 元(30X 修正前 27(F)PER)調低至 2,380 元(22X 修正後 27(F)PER)。

公司簡介 
智邦科技股份有限公司(股票代碼 ：2345)成立於 1988 年 2 月 9 日 ， 主要從事企業級與電信級高
速乙太網路交換器、無線區域網路產品、寬頻產品、消費性電子產品製造業務，近年來積極產品
更新，推出 400G/800G/1.6T Data center 用交換器、Cloud Wi-Fi、Smart Home 等新產品。 
智邦早期經營模式主要替品牌廠商進行純硬體代工 ， 在由品牌廠商銷售給終端客戶 。 近年公司轉
向與軟體廠商進行合作或配合客戶本身設計的軟體 ， 可直接提供終端客戶軟硬體整合性產品 。 早
期做網卡、路由器、中小型伺服器起家，幫HP、Juniper、Huawei 代工，有優於同業之硬體設
計、測試與軟韌體設計能力，是取得資料中心客戶訂單之重要關鍵，一台 100G Switch 要 600
多個零件、測試複雜度高，交期、價格都須符合客戶需求，提高了產業競爭門檻。 
智邦 2Q26 產品營收分類為：Network Application 營收 441.10 億元、Switch 營收 495.39 億
元、Metro Access Switch 營收 9.42 億元、Wireless 營收 7.67 億元、其他服務 1.77 億元；
2Q26 以地區別分類營收為：北美815.58 億元、台灣22.92 億元、歐洲82.71 億元、亞太34.14
億元、其他國家低於百萬元。

智邦 ESG 政策 
1. 節能減碳：由於營運用電為公司排碳主要來源，故 2022 年起執行改善措施，包括細化盤
查，依據廠區使用目的，細化用電分區，設置分區電表，用電資訊上傳雲端，確實掌控用電
狀況及節電績效；生產優化，依據生產機台用電狀況，分析可能的異常，查出異常用電熱
點，予以改善；設備改善，廠區高低溫測試設備，評估測試方式，以減少升溫降溫的能耗。 
2. 董事會多元性方面：公司 2022 年設有八席董事（含獨立董事 5 人、約全體董事席次三分
之二，佔比約 62%） ，都是由各具備多元背景、充足專業知識、經驗與卓越見識並擁有高度
道德標準的業專人士所組成。獨立董事專長領域：2 位台灣籍（財會、營運、危管；行政、
營運、決策） 、1 位美籍（產業、營運、國際） 、1 位日籍（經營、領導、國際） 、1 位以色列
籍（產業、營運、國際） ，超越金管會3 席獨董之規定，確立公司治理專業化、公開化，以
全體股東權益為中心之目標原則，召開 6 次董事會，所有議案均向董事會提案後通過。 
3. ESG 創新：智邦企業永續委員會下設有環境委員會與綠色製造委員會，然建議揭露該部門
職責及是否取得任何綠色認證 。 此外 ， 智邦持續採取在地化採購策略 ， 且揭露在地化採購金
額比重，但建議公司進行綠色採購及發行綠色債券。建議公司強化此構面政策落實並揭露
量化成效，以利更加客觀評斷該公司此構面 ESG 表現。

投資評等定義

英文評等 中文評等 潛在報酬率 
STRONG BUY 強力買進 > +30% 
BUY 買進 介於+15% ~ +30% 
NEUTRAL 中立 介於-15% ~ +15% 
SELL 賣出 < -15% 
註:潛在報酬率=( 6 個月內目標價/本次報告評等最近一天收盤價 – 1 ) * 100

個股與大盤走勢(%)

黎光岱 
David.li@ctbcsis.com

半導體 
盛群 (6202 TT)

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
2H26 營運優於 1H26 ， 安 防 產 品 出 貨 成 長 快 速 
⚫ 透過 MCU 結合周邊 IC 提 供完整解決方案  
⚫ 2H26 營運狀況 將優於 1H26 ，2026 全年營收將成長雙位數  
⚫ 7/31 發布產品價格全面調漲通知，整體毛利率維持在 40% 
透過 MCU 結合周邊 IC 提供完整解決方案： 盛群 營收來自 MCU 比重
達 75%，其餘為周邊 IC(Power 、Display 、記憶體、RF IC、Touch IC 等)。
盛群 MCU 與其他標準 MCU 廠商不同，會針對不同產品與客戶需求，
將周邊 IC 整合到 MCU 內部，可減少外部元件， 讓所需的 PCB 更小 ，
凸顯產品的競爭優勢，如果無法整合到 MCU 內部，也可以透過 MCU
與周邊 IC 搭配，提供完整解決方案。  
2H26 營運狀況將優於 1H26 ，2026 全年營收將成長雙位數： 盛群 MCU
銷售從 2025 年開始主推 32bit ，出貨數量從 1Q25 開始明顯提高， 2H26
營運狀況將優於 1H26，2026 全年營收央成長雙位數。 Touch MCU 成長
主要來自中國客戶訂單回流， 2H26 出貨量將優於 1H26。安防產品整體
訂單能見度較高，與各國政府推出安防新標準相關， 2H26 出貨量將優
於 1H26。 健康產品受季節性因素影響較大 ， 2026 年開始打入韓國市場 ，
主要產品包含八電極體脂秤、血糖儀， 2H26 出貨量將優於 1H26 。馬達
(BLDC) 產品今年將翻倍成 長，2H26 出貨量將優於 1H26 ，在印度市場
除了原有的低壓吊扇 ， 從 2026 年開始拓展到冰箱壓縮機 ， 並有望在 2027
年 有 第 二 個 客 戶 導 入 量 產 ； 台 灣 伺 服 器 散 熱 風 扇 確 定 獲 得 台 灣 大 廠 的
Design -win ，在 9M26 已取得相關訂單 ，預計在 1Q27 開始小量出貨 ，
該案的 終端客戶為國外指標大廠， 盛群 成功取代 原有 國外廠商 (ST) 的方
案，主要優勢在於 提供更低的 價格與 更短的 交期。  
7/31 發布 產品價格 全 面調漲 通知 ，整體 毛利率維持在 40% ：盛群 在
3M26 即有針對部分低毛利率專案調整價格，並在 7/31 發布全面漲
價通知，產品公告漲幅 10~20% ，主要反映封裝與投片成本，目前未
交訂單的價格都已調整，客戶並沒有因為調漲價格而出現轉單的情
況，仍持續有其他新訂 單流入。公司將持續動 態調整各項產品售價，
將整體毛利率維持在 40% 的水準。  
我們基於 2027 年的預估 EPS 3. 45 元*20 倍 PER ，維持 增加持股評等 ，
目標價 69 元。 風險因子 :晶圓廠產能、 MCU 終端 需求 。

公司簡介： 盛群半導體 (Holtek Semicond uctor) 為專業微控制器 (MCU)IC 設計
領導廠商，產品主要包括 8 位元與 32 位元 MCU IC 及其週邊元件之設計、研
發與銷售，總部位於新竹科學園區。自 1998 年成立以來，盛群憑藉其在 8 位
元與 32 位元  MCU 領域的深厚技術，已成為全球工業控制、家電、醫療與安
全防護等應用的關鍵供應商。  
8 位元與 32 位元 MCU 銷售比重： 以 2Q26 出貨數量來看，主要還是以 8 位元
產品為主約 88%，32 位元產品約 12%；以營收金 額 來 看， 8 位 元 產 品約 75% ，
32 位元產品約 25% 。

### Page 1

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

- 1 - 
統新(6426) 
資料中心互連需求使濾光片產線滿載，27 年新增產能，惟
稅率回升壓縮獲利成長 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $321 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.09.30 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI資料中心帶動 DWDM 濾光片需求，統新產線維持滿載，26Q2 毛利率 56.2%，營收規模放
大帶動獲利轉佳；新增約 30%產能預計 27Q1 陸續開出，惟虧損扣抵額度預計 26Q4 用完，
2027 年稅率回升至 20%。預估 2026／2027年 EPS 8.1／10.7 元，給予30倍 2027F PER，
目標價 321 元，潛在上漲空間-9.3%，投資評等為「區間操作」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/09/29 收盤價： 354.00 ➢ 26Q2 營收 2.08 億元（QoQ +34.7%、YoY +82.4%），
毛利率 56.2%、營益率 36.1%，EPS 2.22 元，26H1
累計 EPS 2.96 元；產線維持滿載，研究部預估 26Q3
／26Q4 營收 2.26／2.36 億元，毛利率 60.0%／
58.1%，EPS 2.76／2.33 元。

➢ AI資料中心互連需求使產線維持滿載， 公司優先承接價
格較佳訂單；26H2 仍以既有產能出貨，研究部預估
26H2營收 4.62 億元，較 26H1 成長 27.6%。

➢ 公司規劃增加約 30%產能，27Q1 起陸續貢獻；新增機
台每月折舊約 500 萬元，27Q1 毛利率預估 57.2%，
27Q3 隨稼動率提升回到 58.5%。惟虧損扣抵額度預計
26Q4 用完，2027 年稅率回升至 20%。

➢ 預估統新 2026 年營收 8.2 億元（YoY +69.1%） 、毛利
率 54.7%（年增 24.8ppt）、EPS 8.1 元；2027 年營收
11.7 億元（ YoY +42.3% ） 、毛利率58.0%（年增
3.3ppt）、EPS 10.7 元。統新產品集中於 DWDM 濾光
片，2027 年成長主要對應約 30%新增產能，惟稅率回
升使 EPS 成長低於本業，給予 30 倍 PE，目標價 321
元。潛在上漲空間-9.3%，投資評等為「區間操作」 。

2027(F) 11.7  42.3  4.8  67.3  4.1  32.8  10.70  32.8  0.0  30.5  33.1  8.8  
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
統新為國內少數 DWDM濾光片/光學鍍膜廠商，應用轉向 AI資料中心互聯 
統新 （6426） 主要從事光通訊用濾光片及光學鍍膜元件之研發、生產及銷售，
總部及生產據點位於台南科學園區，2013 年上櫃、2021 年轉上市，股本3.85
億元 。 產品分為被動元件與主動元件 ， 其中被動元件占營收約九成 ， 以DWDM
（高密度分波多工）濾光片為主，應用於資料中心互連及電信網路之波長分
光。 
製程方面，公司採購基板後，於正面堆疊光學膜，背面再鍍抗反射膜提高穿
透率，最後切割為小尺寸成品出貨。DWDM濾光片目前需鍍至約兩百層，層
數越多，單層失敗即造成整片報廢的風險越高；鍍膜過程熱負載易使基板翹
曲，因此須搭配較厚基板及專用切割製程，良率取決於機台、膜層設計與切
割製程的整體調校。主動元件約鍍二三十層即可達到規格，技術門檻相對較
低。 
國內 DWDM濾光片廠商主要為統新及東典 （6588） ，國際同業包括Coherent
（原 II-VI）&Iridian Technologies(加拿大)。公司亦以主動元件機台承接衛星
太陽能電池鍍膜等代工，並將鍍膜技術延伸至生醫、航太、半導體及機器人
等應用，目前營收占比仍低。 
圖一、統新(6426) 2026Q2產品營收比重

資料來源：統新、兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 -

產線滿載帶動 26Q2毛利率升至 56.2%，26H2營收延續成長 
統新 26Q2 營收 2.08 億元，QoQ +34.7%、YoY +82.4%，毛利率 56.2%，
較去年同期增加 36.1ppt，營益率36.1%，EPS 2.22 元；26H1 累計 EPS 2.96
元，去年同期 EPS -1.78 元，已轉虧為盈。 
統新成本以折舊、人事及製造費用等固定支出為主，25Q2 與 26Q2 營業成
本均為 0.91 億元，營收則由1.14 億元增至 2.08 億元，營收增加而成本持平，
毛利率由 20.1%升至 56.2%，營益率由-18.5%轉為 36.1%。產品方面，AI
資料中心互連需求使產線維持滿載，公司依機台適合的規格及訂單價格排
程，優先承接價格較佳的訂單，小量試單則延後排入，有助產品組合改善。
研究部預估 26Q3／26Q4 毛利率 60.0%／58.1%，新機台開出前毛利率維持
高檔，2026 年毛利率 54.7%，較 2025 年的 29.9%增加 24.8ppt。

月營收趨於持平，2027年成長須待新增產能  
7–8月月營收維持在 7,334萬至 7,829萬元，較 26Q2 月均約 6,900 萬元提
升有限，8月並 MoM -6.3%，在產線滿載下月營收已趨持平。需求端客戶訂
單能見度由 2至 3週拉長至 3 至 4個月，美系客戶並以預付訂金確保供貨，
營收成長受限於產能而非訂單。研究部預估 26Q3／26Q4 營收 2.26／2.36
億元，QoQ +9.0%／+4.0%，EPS 2.76／2.33 元，26H2營收成長主要來自
既有產能維持滿載，進一步上修空間有限。在新機台 27Q1 開出前，統新營
收與獲利上限取決於現有產能。 
產能方面，公司規劃增加約 30%產能，設備由既有供應商供貨，26Q2 資本
支出 3,500 萬元。新機台預計 27年初進廠，每台約需一個月調校後量產，
新增產能自 27Q1 陸續貢獻。 
新機台折舊主要認列於營業成本，27Q1 新機台調校期間已開始提列折舊，
產出尚未完全開出，研究部預估毛利率57.2%，低於 26Q4 的 58.1%；27Q3
隨稼動率提升回升至 58.5%，2027 年全年毛利率 58.0%。

虧損扣抵額度 26Q4用完，2027年稅率回升至 20% 
統新過去年度虧損可扣抵 2026 年課稅所得，2026 年稅負較低，研究部預估
扣抵額度於 26Q4 用完，26Q4 所得稅費用 1,300 萬元，2026 年實質稅率約
2.4%。2027年起回歸 20%正常稅率 ， 研究部預估所得稅費用 1.03億元 。2027
年稅前淨利 YoY +62.1%，但稅後淨利 YoY僅+32.8%，稅率回升使 EPS成
長幅度明顯低於本業獲利成長，為研究部評價的主要考量之一。

### Page 4

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預

- 4 - 
圖二、統新(6426)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

新增產能支撐 2027年成長，稅率回升後評價已反映 
評價方面，光通訊族群中具 NPO／CPO 題材之個股評價普遍較高；統新產
品集中於 DWDM濾光片 ， 主要應用於資料中心互連及電信網路 ， 並非純 CPO
題材，營收成長主要取決於機台擴充幅度，加上稅率由約2.4%回升至 20%，
2027 年 EPS 成長 32.8%，低於營業利益成長 67.3%，因此評價較 CPO 相
關個股折價，給予 30倍 2027F PER，目標價 321 元。研究部預估採保守假
設，2027 年營收成長僅反映約 30%新增產能，未納入產品價格調漲及下一
階段擴產，毛利率亦假設維持在 58%左右、低於 26Q3 預估的 60.0%；在客
戶訂單能見度拉長至 3至 4個月、部分客戶預付訂金確保供貨下，實際營收
及獲利有機會優於研究部預估。現價約對應44.0倍 2026F 及 33.1倍 2027F 
PER，已反映 2027 年稅後獲利成長，潛在上漲空間-9.3%，投資評等為「區
間操作」。
